### 1. user (2026-07-04T01:28:06.037Z)

In the khelsutra repo (C:/Users/avidu/Projects/khelsutra-guru/khelsutra), add a client-side upload-size guard to the ingest SPA so an oversized file fails INSTANTLY with a clear, localized message + a "trim/chunk it first" hint — before any network round-trip.

Context (already verified):
- The engine (badminton-highlight-indexer `backend/api/uploads.py`) already fail-fasts: `/upload/init` returns 413 when `total_size_bytes > security.max_upload_mb` (default 4096 MB) BEFORE any part is transferred. So no bytes are wasted today — but the SPA briefly shows "uploading" then flips to a generic "engine" error via `classifyError`, with no size-specific message or chunk hint.
- A companion engine change exposes `max_upload_mb` in `GET /api/capabilities` (see that PR). Consume it here.

Implement in `web/src/`:
1. `api/types.ts` — add `max_upload_mb` to the `EngineCapabilities` type.
2. `lib/errors.ts` — add a `"fileTooLarge"` `IngestErrorCode` (carry the file size + cap so the message can show both).
3. `hooks/useUpload.ts` — `start(file)` already rejects 0-byte files pre-network (line ~38); add a size guard right after it: if `maxUploadBytes && file.size > maxUploadBytes` → `setPhase({status:'error', error:{code:'fileTooLarge', ...}})` and return, no network. Thread the cap in (param or a capabilities context).
4. `components/IngestPanel.tsx` — localize the `fileTooLarge` message with the actual size + cap, e.g. "This clip is 5.8 GB, over the 4 GB upload limit. Trim it to a shorter highlight first — see scripts/chunk_video (or use the Google Drive path)."
5. i18n: add the `fileTooLarge` string to all locale files (the SPA ships ~6 languages — mirror the existing error strings).
6. Tests: extend `components/upload.test.tsx` / the errors test for the new code + the pre-network reject.

Keep `useUpload` i18n-free (return the code, not prose) per its existing contract — IngestPanel maps the code to a localized string. Reference the chunking helper `scripts/chunk_video.py` (added in the engine repo) in the message. Run the SPA test suite + typecheck + lint before opening the PR.

### 7. user (2026-07-04T01:28:35.797Z)

1	// Error helpers shared by the ingest flow and the build flow. Kept i18n-FREE: callers map an
2	// IngestErrorCode to a localized string (so hooks stay testable without a translation context),
3	// while the engine's own `detail` text passes through verbatim (it's the most specific message).
4	import { ApiError } from "../api/client";
5	
6	function isProxyBoilerplate(body: string): boolean {
7	  const s = body.trim().toLowerCase();
8	  if (!s) return false;
9	  return (
10	    s.startsWith("<!doctype html") ||
11	    s.startsWith("<html") ||
12	    /<html[\s>]/i.test(body) ||
13	    /<\/html>/i.test(body) ||
14	    s.includes("error occurred while trying to proxy") ||
15	    (s.includes("cloudflare") && s.includes("error"))
16	  );
17	}
18	
19	/** Pull FastAPI's {"detail": "..."} message out of an error body, else return safe plain text. */
20	export function parseDetail(body: string): string {
21	  try {
22	    const j = JSON.parse(body) as { detail?: unknown };
23	    if (j && typeof j.detail === "string") return j.detail;
24	  } catch {
25	    /* not JSON — fall through to safe plain text handling */
26	  }
27	  if (isProxyBoilerplate(body)) return "";
28	  return body;
29	}
30	
31	/** Stable, localizable failure categories surfaced by the ingest flow.
32	 *  `engine` carries the engine's own message in `detail`; the rest are localized by code. */
33	export type IngestErrorCode = "invalidUri" | "networkError" | "emptyResult" | "timeout" | "engine";
34	
35	export interface IngestError {
36	  code: IngestErrorCode;
37	  /** Present for `engine` — the verbatim FastAPI detail / status; rendered as-is, not translated. */
38	  detail?: string;
39	}
40	
41	/** Classify a thrown client error into a stable code. A network throw (ApiError with no `status`)
42	 *  is the common "engine unreachable" case → its own friendly code; any HTTP error surfaces the
43	 *  engine's `detail` (e.g. an unsupported-scheme 400, a not-found 404). */
44	export function classifyError(err: unknown): IngestError {
45	  if (err instanceof ApiError) {
46	    if (err.status === undefined) return { code: "networkError" }; // fetch threw — engine truly unreachable
47	    if (err.body) {
48	      const detail = parseDetail(err.body);
49	      if (detail) return { code: "engine", detail }; // a real engine message (e.g. a 400 unsupported-scheme)
50	    }
51	    // A bare 5xx with no body is almost always the engine being down / a gateway that can't reach it
52	    // (e.g. the dev proxy when :8000 is off) — for a non-technical user a friendly "is it running?"
53	    // beats a cryptic "HTTP 500". A 4xx with no body keeps the status (it's a client-side mistake).
54	    if (err.status >= 500) return { code: "networkError" };
55	    return { code: "engine", detail: `HTTP ${err.status}` };
56	  }
57	  return { code: "engine", detail: String(err) };
58	}
59

### 8. user (2026-07-04T01:28:36.793Z)

1	// Drives the in-UI file UPLOAD half of ingest (tracker T4): chunk a local file up to the engine's
2	// /api/upload/* endpoints, report byte progress, then hand the returned server PATH to the existing
3	// processing back-half (useIngestJob.submit). Alpha users have a FILE, not a gs:// URI — this is the
4	// keystone that lets them point the tool at it from the browser.
5	//
6	// Kept i18n-FREE (returns stable IngestErrorCodes via classifyError, not prose) so it unit-tests
7	// without a translation context — IngestPanel maps codes to localized strings via errorText. Abort
8	// is an AbortController: cancel/unmount/a superseding pick stops the in-flight upload and the
9	// orchestrator best-effort DELETEs the server-side partial.
10	import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
11	import { uploadFile } from "../api/client";
12	import { classifyError, type IngestError } from "../lib/errors";
13	import { log, newCorrelationId } from "../lib/log";
14	
15	export type UploadPhase =
16	  | { status: "idle" }
17	  | { status: "uploading"; pct: number; filename: string }
18	  | { status: "error"; error: IngestError };
19	
20	export function useUpload(onComplete: (path: string, filename: string) => void) {
21	  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<UploadPhase>({ status: "idle" });
22	  const ctrlRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);
23	  // Keep the latest onComplete without making start depend on it (a parent re-render won't restart).
24	  const onCompleteRef = useRef(onComplete);
25	  onCompleteRef.current = onComplete;
26	
27	  const stop = useCallback(() => {
28	    ctrlRef.current?.abort();
29	    ctrlRef.current = null;
30	  }, []);
31	
32	  // Abort any in-flight upload on unmount.
33	  useEffect(() => stop, [stop]);
34	
35	  const start = useCallback(
36	    (file: File) => {
37	      // A 0-byte "video" is never valid — reject before touching the network (reuse invalidUri copy).
38	      if (!file || file.size === 0) {
39	        setPhase({ status: "error", error: { code: "invalidUri" } });
40	        return;
41	      }
42	
43	      stop(); // supersede any prior upload
44	      const ctrl = new AbortController();
45	      ctrlRef.current = ctrl;
46	      const cid = newCorrelationId();
47	      setPhase({ status: "uploading", pct: 0, filename: file.name });
48	      log("info", "upload.submit_start", { cid, filename: file.name, size: file.size });
49	
50	      void (async () => {
51	        try {
52	          const res = await uploadFile(file, {
53	            cid,
54	            signal: ctrl.signal,
55	            onProgress: ({ sentBytes, totalBytes }) => {
56	              if (ctrl.signal.aborted) return;
57	              const pct = totalBytes > 0 ? Math.min(100, Math.round((sentBytes / totalBytes) * 100)) : 0;
58	              setPhase({ status: "uploading", pct, filename: file.name });
59	            },
60	          });
61	          if (ctrl.signal.aborted) return;
62	          setPhase({ status: "idle" }); // hand off — the engine processing phase takes over
63	          onCompleteRef.current(res.path, file.name);
64	        } catch (err) {
65	          // User-cancelled (abort) is silent, not an error.
66	          if (ctrl.signal.aborted || (err instanceof DOMException && err.name === "AbortError")) return;
67	          setPhase({ status: "error", error: classifyError(err) });
68	          log("error", "upload.failed", { cid, filename: file.name, error: String(err) });
69	        }
70	      })();
71	    },
72	    [stop],
73	  );
74	
75	  const cancel = useCallback(() => {
76	    stop();
77	    setPhase({ status: "idle" });
78	    log("info", "upload.cancel", {});
79	  }, [stop]);
80	
81	  const reset = useCallback(() => {
82	    stop();
83	    setPhase({ status: "idle" });
84	  }, [stop]);
85	
86	  const busy = phase.status === "uploading";
87	  return { phase, start, cancel, reset, busy };
88	}
89

1	// The front-half ingest screen (appliance S1/S2; tracker B-P2 + T4). Two ways to point the tool at a
2	// video: UPLOAD a local file (primary — alpha users have a file, not a URI; chunked to the engine's
3	// /api/upload/* endpoints) or paste a `gs://` bucket URI / host path (secondary). Both converge on
4	// the SAME back-half: POST /api/process → poll GET /api/jobs/{id} → hand the video_id to App. Honest
5	// by construction: the engine reports coarse phase WORDS (not a %), and a success-shaped failure
6	// (done but no rallies) is shown, never silently treated as a load.
7	import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
8	import type { ChangeEvent, FormEvent } from "react";
9	import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
10	import { createAsset, getCapabilities } from "../api/client";
11	import type { StorageBackendInfo } from "../api/types";
12	import { useIngestJob } from "../hooks/useIngestJob";
13	import { useUpload } from "../hooks/useUpload";
14	import { classifyError, type IngestError } from "../lib/errors";
15	import { errorText } from "../lib/errorText";
16	import { log, newCorrelationId } from "../lib/log";
17	import { StatusChip, Spinner } from "./StatusChip";
18	
19	type Compute = "local" | "cloud";
20	// The three storage-medium choices the picker offers. "bucket" covers s3 OR gcs (the user pastes a
21	// full scheme'd URI); "drive" is a mounted Google Drive folder (STUDIO_MEDIA_LIFECYCLE.md P5, D14).
22	type Medium = "local" | "bucket" | "drive";
23	
24	interface Props {
25	  /** Called with the engine's video_id once a submitted job finishes successfully. */
26	  onLoaded: (videoId: string, displayName?: string) => void;
27	  /** Lets the shell hide demo-only controls while a real ingest is in flight. */
28	  onBusyChange?: (busy: boolean) => void;
29	}
30	
31	function formatElapsed(totalSec: number): string {
32	  const sec = Math.max(0, Math.floor(totalSec));
33	  const mm = Math.floor(sec / 60);
34	  const ss = sec % 60;
35	  return `${mm}:${String(ss).padStart(2, "0")}`;
36	}
37	
38	function sourceLabel(value: string): string | null {
39	  const trimmed = value.trim();
40	  if (!trimmed) return null;
41	  try {
42	    const parsed = new URL(trimmed);
43	    const tail = parsed.pathname.split(/[\\/]/).filter(Boolean).pop();
44	    return tail ? decodeURIComponent(tail) : trimmed;
45	  } catch {
46	    return trimmed.split(/[\\/]/).filter(Boolean).pop() ?? trimmed;
47	  }
48	}
49	
50	export function IngestPanel({ onLoaded, onBusyChange }: Props) {
51	  const { t, i18n } = useTranslation();
52	  const [uri, setUri] = useState("");
53	  const [activeSource, setActiveSource] = useState<string | null>(null);
54	  const { phase, submit, cancel: cancelIngest, reset: resetIngest, busy: ingestBusy } = useIngestJob(onLoaded);
55	  // The UI locale recorded with the submission (PMF analytics + the localized reel watermark).
56	  const locale = i18n.resolvedLanguage ?? i18n.language;
57	
58	  // Compute-picker (PACKAGING compute-picker, item 3): if this server can dispatch to a cloud GPU
59	  // (capabilities.deployment.cloud_available), let the user choose where DETECT runs. Default LOCAL
60	  // (free, no surprise cost); CLOUD requires an explicit cost acknowledgement before it can run.
61	  const [cloudAvailable, setCloudAvailable] = useState(false);
62	  const [compute, setCompute] = useState<Compute>("local");
63	  const [cloudAck, setCloudAck] = useState(false);
64	  // Storage-medium picker (P5): which media THIS box can store into (capabilities.storage.backends,
65	  // usable-only). Absent/local-only ⇒ no picker (today's flow unchanged).
66	  const [backends, setBackends] = useState<StorageBackendInfo[]>([]);
67	  const [medium, setMedium] = useState<Medium>("local");
68	  const [mediumUri, setMediumUri] = useState("");
69	  const [storing, setStoring] = useState(false);
70	  const [storeError, setStoreError] = useState<IngestError | null>(null);
71	  useEffect(() => {
72	    let alive = true;
73	    getCapabilities()
74	      .then((c) => {
75	        if (!alive) return;
76	        setCloudAvailable(c.deployment?.cloud_available === true);
77	        setBackends(c.storage?.backends ?? []);
78	      })
79	      .catch((err) => log("warn", "capabilities.unavailable", { error: String(err) }));
80	    return () => {
81	      alive = false;
82	    };
83	  }, []);
84	  // Only send a choice when the picker is actually shown; otherwise omit it (⇒ server default).
85	  const sendCompute = cloudAvailable ? compute : undefined;
86	  // CLOUD selected but the cost note not yet accepted → block submit (don't silently run local, and
87	  // don't dispatch a paid job without consent).
88	  const cloudBlocked = cloudAvailable && compute === "cloud" && !cloudAck;
89	
90	  // Which medium segments to offer: local always; "bucket" if s3 OR gcs is usable; "drive" if a Drive
91	  // mount is usable. The picker only appears when there's a REAL choice (a non-local usable backend).
92	  const canUse = (id: string) => backends.some((b) => b.id === id && b.usable);
93	  const canBucket = canUse("s3") || canUse("gcs");
94	  const canDrive = canUse("gdrive");
95	  const mediumOptions: Medium[] = ["local", ...(canBucket ? ["bucket" as const] : []), ...(canDrive ? ["drive" as const] : [])];
96	  const showMediumPicker = mediumOptions.length > 1;
97	  const needsMediumUri = medium !== "local";
98	  // A non-local medium needs a destination URI before anything can be stored/submitted.
99	  const mediumBlocked = needsMediumUri && mediumUri.trim() === "";
100	
101	  // Store the obtained source (an uploaded local path, or a pasted at-rest URI) INTO the chosen medium,
102	  // then process the durable copy. A failed store is loud (surfaced like every other ingest error).
103	  const storeThenProcess = async (src: { uploadedPath?: string; sourceUri?: string }, displayName?: string) => {
104	    const cid = newCorrelationId();
105	    setStoreError(null);
106	    setStoring(true);
107	    try {
108	      const asset = await createAsset({ ...src, mediumUri: mediumUri.trim() }, cid);
109	      log("info", "asset.stored", { cid, video_id: asset.video_id, medium: asset.medium, copied: asset.copied });
110	      submit(asset.stored_uri, locale, sendCompute, displayName ?? sourceLabel(asset.stored_uri) ?? undefined); // index the stored copy
111	    } catch (err) {
112	      setStoreError(classifyError(err));
113	      log("error", "asset.store_failed", { cid, error: String(err) });
114	    } finally {
115	      setStoring(false);
116	    }
117	  };
118	
119	  // On upload completion: local medium → process the upload directly (today's flow); a non-local
120	  // medium → store a durable copy into it first, then process. The arrow is recreated each render
121	  // (useUpload reads onCompleteRef.current), so `medium`/`mediumUri`/`sendCompute` are always fresh.
122	  const upload = useUpload((path, filename) => {
123	    if (medium === "local") submit(path, locale, sendCompute, filename);
124	    else void storeThenProcess({ uploadedPath: path }, filename);
125	  });
126	  const busy = ingestBusy || upload.busy || storing;
127	  useEffect(() => {
128	    onBusyChange?.(busy);
129	    return () => {
130	      if (busy) onBusyChange?.(false);
131	    };
132	  }, [busy, onBusyChange]);
133	  // Disable the submit surfaces while busy, awaiting the cloud cost ack, or missing a medium URI.
134	  const inputsDisabled = busy || cloudBlocked || mediumBlocked;
135	
136	  const onSubmitUri = (e: FormEvent) => {
137	    e.preventDefault();
138	    if (inputsDisabled) return;
139	    // The URI form is the SOURCE only in local mode (a bucket URL / host path to index directly). For
140	    // a non-local medium the source is the upload; the text box below is the DESTINATION, not this.
141	    const displayName = sourceLabel(uri);
142	    setActiveSource(displayName);
143	    submit(uri, locale, sendCompute, displayName ?? undefined);
144	  };
145	  const onFile = (e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
146	    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
147	    // Symmetric with onSubmitUri: never start while blocked (the disabled input already prevents this).
148	    if (file && !inputsDisabled) {
149	      setActiveSource(file.name);
150	      upload.start(file);
151	    }
152	    e.target.value = ""; // let re-picking the same file fire change again
153	  };
154	  const cancelAll = () => {
155	    upload.cancel();
156	    cancelIngest();
157	    setActiveSource(null);
158	  };
159	  const resetAll = () => {
160	    upload.reset();
161	    resetIngest();
162	    setStoreError(null);
163	    setActiveSource(null);
164	  };
165	
166	  // Shared mapping with the build flow (lib/errorText): an `engine` failure carries the engine's own
167	  // message (a 400 scheme/path/extension detail, a worker error); every other code maps to a friendly
168	  // localized line. Surface whichever half failed (upload or engine) — they're mutually exclusive.
169	  const errorState =
170	    upload.phase.status === "error"
171	      ? upload.phase.error
172	      : storeError ?? (phase.status === "error" ? phase.error : null);
173	  const errorMsg = errorState ? errorText(t, errorState) : null;
174	  const visibleSource = upload.phase.status === "uploading" ? upload.phase.filename : activeSource;
175	  const processingName =
176	    phase.status === "submitting" || phase.status === "polling" ? phase.displayName : undefined;
177	
178	  return (
179	    <section className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-black/10 bg-white p-5" data-testid="ingest-panel">
180	      <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">{t("ingest.title")}</h2>
181	      <p className="mt-1 max-w-2xl text-sm text-ink/60">{t("ingest.intro")}</p>
182	
183	      {/* COMPUTE PICKER (item 3): shown only when this server can dispatch to a cloud GPU. Default
184	          LOCAL; CLOUD reveals a cost note + a required acknowledgement before any submit can run. */}
185	      {cloudAvailable && (
186	        <fieldset className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-black/10 p-3" data-testid="compute-picker">
187	          <legend className="px-1 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-ink/40">
188	            {t("ingest.compute.legend")}
189	          </legend>
190	          <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
191	            {(["local", "cloud"] as const).map((opt) => (
192	              <button
193	                key={opt}
194	                type="button"
195	                aria-pressed={compute === opt}
196	                disabled={busy}
197	                onClick={() => setCompute(opt)}
198	                className={
199	                  "min-h-11 rounded-xl border px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold disabled:opacity-40 " +
200	                  (compute === opt
201	                    ? "border-green bg-green/10 text-ink"
202	                    : "border-black/15 text-ink/70 hover:border-green")
203	                }
204	              >
205	                {t(`ingest.compute.${opt}`)}
206	              </button>
207	            ))}
208	          </div>
209	          {compute === "cloud" && (
210	            <div className="mt-3" data-testid="compute-cost">
211	              <p className="text-xs text-ink/60">{t("ingest.compute.cloudCost")}</p>
212	              <label className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-ink/80">
213	                <input
214	                  type="checkbox"
215	                  checked={cloudAck}
216	                  disabled={busy}
217	                  onChange={(e) => setCloudAck(e.target.checked)}
218	                  className="h-4 w-4"
219	                />
220	                {t("ingest.compute.ack")}
221	              </label>
222	              {cloudBlocked && (
223	                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-amber-700" data-testid="compute-ack-required">
224	                  {t("ingest.compute.ackRequired")}
225	                </p>
226	              )}
227	            </div>
228	          )}
229	        </fieldset>
230	      )}
231	
232	      {/* MEDIUM PICKER (P5, D14): where to STORE this video. Rendered only from the media this box can
233	          actually use (capabilities.storage.backends, usable-only), and only when there's a real
234	          choice beyond local. A non-local medium reveals a destination-URI field; the video is stored
235	          there (a durable, MD5-keyed copy — free-space checked) and then indexed. Self-host posture. */}
236	      {showMediumPicker && (
237	        <fieldset className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-black/10 p-3" data-testid="medium-picker">
238	          <legend className="px-1 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-ink/40">
239	            {t("ingest.medium.legend")}
240	          </legend>
241	          <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
242	            {mediumOptions.map((opt) => (
243	              <button
244	                key={opt}
245	                type="button"
246	                aria-pressed={medium === opt}
247	                disabled={busy}
248	                onClick={() => {
249	                  setMedium(opt);
250	                  setStoreError(null);
251	                }}
252	                data-testid={`medium-${opt}`}
253	                className={
254	                  "min-h-11 rounded-xl border px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold disabled:opacity-40 " +
255	                  (medium === opt
256	                    ? "border-green bg-green/10 text-ink"
257	                    : "border-black/15 text-ink/70 hover:border-green")
258	                }
259	              >
260	                {t(`storage.${opt}`)}
261	              </button>
262	            ))}
263	          </div>
264	          {needsMediumUri && (
265	            <div className="mt-3" data-testid="medium-dest">
266	              <input
267	                type="text"
268	                value={mediumUri}
269	                onChange={(e) => setMediumUri(e.target.value)}
270	                placeholder={medium === "drive" ? t("ingest.medium.drivePlaceholder") : t("ingest.medium.bucketPlaceholder")}
271	                aria-label={t("ingest.medium.destAria")}
272	                disabled={busy}
273	                className="min-h-11 w-full max-w-md rounded-xl border border-black/15 bg-white px-4 py-2 text-sm text-ink outline-none focus:border-green disabled:opacity-50"
274	              />
275	              <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-ink/50">{t("ingest.medium.destHint")}</p>
276	              {mediumBlocked && (
277	                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-amber-700" data-testid="medium-required">
278	                  {t("ingest.medium.destRequired")}
279	                </p>
280	              )}
281	            </div>
282	          )}
283	        </fieldset>
284	      )}
285	
286	      {/* PRIMARY: upload a local file (T4). A native file input, visually a button (≥44px, A4). */}
287	      <div className="mt-4">
288	        <label
289	          className={
290	            // focus-within makes the visible button show a focus ring when the (sr-only) input is
291	            // focused — a keyboard user needs a visible focus indicator (WCAG 2.4.7).
292	            "inline-flex min-h-11 items-center rounded-xl bg-ink px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-ink2 focus-within:outline-none focus-within:ring-2 focus-within:ring-green focus-within:ring-offset-2 " +
293	            (inputsDisabled ? "pointer-events-none opacity-40" : "cursor-pointer")
294	          }
295	        >
296	          {t("ingest.chooseFile")}
297	          <input
298	            type="file"
299	            accept=".mp4,.mov,video/mp4,video/quicktime"
300	            aria-label={t("ingest.uploadAria")}
301	            disabled={inputsDisabled}
302	            onChange={onFile}
303	            className="sr-only"
304	          />
305	        </label>
306	        <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-ink/40">{t("ingest.fileHint")}</p>
307	      </div>
308	
309	      {/* SECONDARY: in LOCAL mode, point at a cloud bucket / host path to index directly. Hidden for a
310	          non-local medium, where the destination field above owns the URI box and the upload is the
311	          source (avoids two conflicting URI inputs). */}
312	      {medium === "local" && (
313	      <div className="mt-4 border-t border-black/5 pt-4">
314	        <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-ink/40">{t("ingest.orLink")}</p>
315	        <form onSubmit={onSubmitUri} className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
316	          <input
317	            type="text"
318	            value={uri}
319	            onChange={(e) => setUri(e.target.value)}
320	            placeholder={t("ingest.uriPlaceholder")}
321	            aria-label={t("ingest.uriAriaLabel")}
322	            disabled={inputsDisabled}
323	            className="min-h-11 min-w-[18rem] flex-1 rounded-xl border border-black/15 bg-white px-4 py-2 text-sm text-ink outline-none focus:border-green disabled:opacity-50"
324	          />
325	          <button
326	            type="submit"
327	            disabled={inputsDisabled}
328	            className="min-h-11 rounded-xl bg-ink px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white disabled:opacity-40"
329	          >
330	            {phase.status === "submitting" ? t("ingest.submitting") : t("ingest.submit")}
331	          </button>
332	        </form>
333	        <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-ink/40">{t("ingest.hint")}</p>
334	      </div>
335	      )}
336	
337	      {/* Live status (aria-live so screen readers hear progress) as icon+text chips — never icon-only
338	          (A8). Upload shows a determinate % bar; the engine phase is indeterminate (phase words). */}
339	      <div aria-live="polite" className="mt-3 text-sm" data-testid="ingest-status">
340	        {upload.phase.status === "uploading" && (
341	          <div data-testid="upload-progress">
342	            {/* Static chip label (not the per-tick %) so the aria-live region announces once; the live
343	                % rides the progressbar's aria-valuenow + a sighted-only label (A8/T4). */}
344	            <StatusChip tone="info" icon={<Spinner />} label={t("ingest.uploadingStatus")} />
345	            <p className="mt-2 text-xs font-medium text-ink/70" data-testid="ingest-source">
346	              {t("ingest.sourceFile", { filename: upload.phase.filename })}
347	            </p>
348	            <div className="mt-2 flex max-w-sm items-center gap-2">
349	              <div
350	                className="h-1.5 flex-1 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-black/10"
351	                role="progressbar"
352	                aria-label={t("ingest.uploadProgress")}
353	                aria-valuenow={upload.phase.pct}
354	                aria-valuemin={0}
355	                aria-valuemax={100}
356	              >
357	                <div className="h-full bg-green transition-all" style={{ width: `${upload.phase.pct}%` }} />
358	              </div>
359	              <span aria-hidden="true" className="text-xs tabular-nums text-ink/50">
360	                {upload.phase.pct}%
361	              </span>
362	            </div>
363	            <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-ink/50">{t("ingest.reassure")}</p>
364	            <button
365	              type="button"
366	              onClick={cancelAll}
367	              className="mt-2 inline-flex min-h-11 items-center rounded-xl border border-black/15 px-4 text-sm hover:border-green"
368	            >
369	              {t("ingest.cancel")}
370	            </button>
371	          </div>
372	        )}
373	        {storing && (
374	          <div data-testid="ingest-storing">
375	            <StatusChip tone="info" icon={<Spinner />} label={t("ingest.medium.storing")} />
376	            {visibleSource && (
377	              <p className="mt-2 text-xs font-medium text-ink/70" data-testid="ingest-source">
378	                {t("ingest.sourceFile", { filename: visibleSource })}
379	              </p>
380	            )}
381	          </div>
382	        )}
383	        {ingestBusy && (
384	          <div>
385	            <StatusChip
386	              tone="info"
387	              icon={<Spinner />}
388	              label={
389	                t("ingest.working") +
390	                (processingName ? ` · ${processingName}` : "") +
391	                (phase.status === "polling" && phase.progress
392	                  ? ` · ${t("ingest.progress", { progress: phase.progress })}`
393	                  : "")
394	              }
395	            />
396	            {visibleSource && (
397	              <p className="mt-2 text-xs font-medium text-ink/70" data-testid="ingest-source">
398	                {t("ingest.sourceFile", { filename: visibleSource })}
399	              </p>
400	            )}
401	            {phase.status === "polling" && (
402	              <div className="mt-2 space-y-1 text-xs text-ink/50" data-testid="ingest-heartbeat">
403	                {phase.resumed && <p>{t("ingest.resumeNotice")}</p>}
404	                <p>
405	                  {t("ingest.elapsed", { elapsed: formatElapsed(phase.elapsedSec) })} · {t("ingest.heartbeat")}
406	                </p>
407	                <p>{t("ingest.reloadHint")}</p>
408	              </div>
409	            )}
410	            {/* Reassurance for slow uplinks: the job runs on the server, so it's safe to wait. */}
411	            <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-ink/50">{t("ingest.reassure")}</p>
412	            <button
413	              type="button"
414	              onClick={cancelAll}
415	              className="mt-2 inline-flex min-h-11 items-center rounded-xl border border-black/15 px-4 text-sm hover:border-green"
416	            >
417	              {t("ingest.cancel")}
418	            </button>
419	          </div>
420	        )}
421	        {errorMsg && (
422	          <div className="rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-red-800" data-testid="ingest-error">
423	            <StatusChip tone="error" icon="⚠" label={t("ingest.statusFailed")} />
424	            <p className="mt-2">{errorMsg}</p>
425	            <button
426	              type="button"
427	              onClick={resetAll}
428	              className="mt-2 inline-flex min-h-11 items-center rounded-lg border border-red-300 px-3 text-sm hover:bg-red-100"
429	            >
430	              {t("ingest.retry")}
431	            </button>
432	          </div>
433	        )}
434	      </div>
435	    </section>
436	  );
437	}

### 11. user (2026-07-04T01:28:42.565Z)

1	// The ONE place a classified error (lib/errors.ts) becomes user-facing text. Both surfaces use it:
2	// the ingest flow (IngestPanel) and the build flow (App.onBuild → CompileResult), so a 5xx / engine-
3	// down failure reads the same friendly way everywhere instead of a raw "HTTP 500" / fetch message.
4	//
5	// `engine` carries the engine's OWN message (the most specific) → shown verbatim, never translated.
6	// Every other code maps to a friendly localized line in the shared `errors.*` namespace. Kept out of
7	// errors.ts so that module stays i18n-free and unit-testable without a translation context.
8	import type { TFunction } from "i18next";
9	import type { IngestError } from "./errors";
10	
11	export function errorText(t: TFunction, e: IngestError): string {
12	  if (e.code === "engine") return e.detail ?? t("errors.engineGeneric");
13	  return t(`errors.${e.code}`);
14	}
15

1	// @vitest-environment jsdom
2	//
3	// errorText is the single mapping from a classified error → user-facing text, shared by the ingest
4	// and build flows (A5). These pin: the engine message passes through verbatim, a detail-less engine
5	// error degrades to a friendly localized line (never a raw key), and every non-engine code resolves
6	// to a real catalog string in the shared `errors.*` namespace.
7	import { describe, it, expect, beforeAll } from "vitest";
8	import i18n from "../i18n";
9	import { errorText } from "./errorText";
10	import type { IngestErrorCode } from "./errors";
11	
12	beforeAll(async () => {
13	  await i18n.changeLanguage("en");
14	});
15	
16	describe("errorText", () => {
17	  it("an engine error shows the engine's own detail verbatim (most specific message)", () => {
18	    expect(errorText(i18n.t, { code: "engine", detail: "min_shot_count too low" })).toBe("min_shot_count too low");
19	  });
20	
21	  it("an engine error with no detail degrades to a friendly localized line (not a raw key)", () => {
22	    const s = errorText(i18n.t, { code: "engine" });
23	    expect(s).toBe(i18n.t("errors.engineGeneric"));
24	    expect(s).not.toMatch(/^errors\./);
25	    expect(s.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
26	  });
27	
28	  it("networkError maps to the friendly 'is it running?' line", () => {
29	    expect(errorText(i18n.t, { code: "networkError" })).toMatch(/is it running/i);
30	  });
31	
32	  it("every non-engine code resolves to a real catalog string (never a raw key)", () => {
33	    for (const code of ["invalidUri", "networkError", "emptyResult", "timeout"] as IngestErrorCode[]) {
34	      const s = errorText(i18n.t, { code });
35	      expect(s, code).not.toMatch(/^errors\./);
36	      expect(s.length, code).toBeGreaterThan(0);
37	    }
38	  });
39	
40	  it("localizes per the active locale (Kannada network message)", async () => {
41	    await i18n.changeLanguage("kn");
42	    expect(errorText(i18n.t, { code: "networkError" })).toBe("KhelSutra ತಲುಪಲಾಗುತ್ತಿಲ್ಲ. ಅದು ಚಾಲನೆಯಲ್ಲಿದೆಯೇ?");
43	    await i18n.changeLanguage("en");
44	  });
45	});

1	import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
2	import { parseDetail, classifyError } from "./errors";
3	import { ApiError } from "../api/client";
4	
5	describe("parseDetail", () => {
6	  it("extracts FastAPI's {detail}", () => {
7	    expect(parseDetail(JSON.stringify({ detail: "unsupported storage URI scheme" }))).toBe(
8	      "unsupported storage URI scheme",
9	    );
10	  });
11	  it("returns the raw body when it isn't JSON or has no string detail", () => {
12	    expect(parseDetail("plain text")).toBe("plain text");
13	    expect(parseDetail(JSON.stringify({ other: 1 }))).toBe(JSON.stringify({ other: 1 }));
14	    expect(parseDetail(JSON.stringify({ detail: { nested: true } }))).toBe(JSON.stringify({ detail: { nested: true } }));
15	  });
16	  it("suppresses proxy HTML/text boilerplate instead of treating it as an engine detail", () => {
17	    expect(parseDetail("<!DOCTYPE html><html><title>502 Bad Gateway</title></html>")).toBe("");
18	    expect(parseDetail("Error occurred while trying to proxy: localhost:5173/api/query")).toBe("");
19	  });
20	});
21	
22	describe("classifyError", () => {
23	  it("a network throw (ApiError with no status) → networkError", () => {
24	    expect(classifyError(new ApiError("net", { cid: "c" }))).toEqual({ code: "networkError" });
25	  });
26	  it("an HTTP error with a body → engine + the FastAPI detail", () => {
27	    const err = new ApiError("x", { status: 400, body: JSON.stringify({ detail: "unsupported storage URI scheme" }) });
28	    expect(classifyError(err)).toEqual({ code: "engine", detail: "unsupported storage URI scheme" });
29	  });
30	  it("a bare 5xx with no body → networkError (friendly 'is the engine running?', not 'HTTP 500')", () => {
31	    expect(classifyError(new ApiError("x", { status: 500 }))).toEqual({ code: "networkError" });
32	    expect(classifyError(new ApiError("x", { status: 502 }))).toEqual({ code: "networkError" });
33	  });
34	  it("a 5xx WITH a detail still surfaces the engine's message", () => {
35	    const err = new ApiError("x", { status: 500, body: JSON.stringify({ detail: "failed to persist job record" }) });
36	    expect(classifyError(err)).toEqual({ code: "engine", detail: "failed to persist job record" });
37	  });
38	  it("a 5xx proxy HTML body → networkError, not raw markup in the UI", () => {
39	    const err = new ApiError("x", { status: 502, body: "<html><body>Bad Gateway</body></html>" });
40	    expect(classifyError(err)).toEqual({ code: "networkError" });
41	  });
42	  it("a 5xx proxy text body → networkError, not a proxy implementation detail", () => {
43	    const err = new ApiError("x", { status: 500, body: "Error occurred while trying to proxy: localhost:5173/api/process" });
44	    expect(classifyError(err)).toEqual({ code: "networkError" });
45	  });
46	  it("a 4xx with no body keeps the status (a client-side mistake, not unreachable)", () => {
47	    expect(classifyError(new ApiError("x", { status: 404 }))).toEqual({ code: "engine", detail: "HTTP 404" });
48	  });
49	  it("a non-ApiError → engine + the stringified error", () => {
50	    expect(classifyError(new Error("boom"))).toEqual({ code: "engine", detail: "Error: boom" });
51	  });
52	});

### 14. user (2026-07-04T01:28:44.923Z)

1	// @vitest-environment jsdom
2	//
3	// T4 end to end through IngestPanel: pick a local file → it chunks up to /api/upload/* → the returned
4	// server PATH is fed to POST /api/process → poll → onLoaded(video_id). The keystone for alpha users
5	// who have a FILE, not a gs:// URI. Also pins that the file picker is present + labelled (a11y/A4).
6	import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach, afterEach } from "vitest";
7	import { render, screen, cleanup, fireEvent, act } from "@testing-library/react";
8	import i18n from "../i18n";
9	import { IngestPanel } from "./IngestPanel";
10	
11	// Flush promise microtasks (the upload/process awaits) under fake timers — waitFor can't advance them.
12	async function flush(times = 15) {
13	  for (let i = 0; i < times; i += 1) {
14	    await act(async () => {
15	      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
16	    });
17	  }
18	}
19	
20	const MB = 1024 * 1024;
21	const ok = (j: unknown) => ({ ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => j, text: async () => JSON.stringify(j) });
22	
23	let processBody: { video_path?: string } | null = null;
24	
25	function uploadThenProcessFetch() {
26	  processBody = null;
27	  return vi.fn(async (url: string | URL, init?: RequestInit) => {
28	    const u = String(url);
29	    const m = init?.method ?? "GET";
30	    if (u === "/api/upload/init") return ok({ upload_id: "up1", max_part_mb: 1, next_part: "/api/upload/up1/part/0" });
31	    if (u.includes("/api/upload/up1/part/")) return ok({ upload_id: "up1", received_parts: 1, received_bytes: (init?.body as Blob).size });
32	    if (u.endsWith("/complete")) return ok({ path: "/srv/uploads/match.mp4", video_id: "PRE", size_bytes: 1 });
33	    if (u === "/api/process" && m === "POST") {
34	      processBody = JSON.parse(init!.body as string);
35	      return ok({ job_id: "j", status: "queued", poll: "/api/jobs/j" });
36	    }
37	    if (u.startsWith("/api/jobs/")) return ok({ job_id: "j", status: "done", video_id: "VIDFINAL", result: { status: "success", video_id: "VIDFINAL" } });
38	    throw new Error(`unexpected fetch ${m} ${u}`);
39	  }) as unknown as typeof fetch;
40	}
41	
42	const videoFile = (bytes: number) => new File([new Uint8Array(bytes)], "match.mp4", { type: "video/mp4" });
43	
44	const pickFile = (file: File) => {
45	  const input = screen.getByLabelText("Choose a video file to upload");
46	  Object.defineProperty(input, "files", { value: [file], configurable: true });
47	  fireEvent.change(input);
48	};
49	
50	beforeEach(async () => {
51	  window.localStorage.clear();
52	  await i18n.changeLanguage("en");
53	});
54	
55	afterEach(() => {
56	  cleanup();
57	  vi.restoreAllMocks();
58	  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
59	  vi.useRealTimers();
60	});
61	
62	describe("T4: in-UI file upload", () => {
63	  it("offers a labelled file picker with a visible keyboard focus ring (WCAG 2.4.7)", () => {
64	    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", vi.fn());
65	    render(<IngestPanel onLoaded={vi.fn()} />);
66	    const input = screen.getByLabelText("Choose a video file to upload");
67	    expect(input).toBeTruthy();
68	    expect(screen.getByText("Choose a video file")).toBeTruthy();
69	    // The sr-only input's focus must surface on the visible label (focus-within ring).
70	    expect(input.closest("label")?.className).toMatch(/focus-within:ring/);
71	  });
72	
73	  it("uploads the picked file, feeds the server path to /api/process, and loads the resulting video_id", async () => {
74	    vi.useFakeTimers();
75	    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", uploadThenProcessFetch());
76	    const onLoaded = vi.fn();
77	    render(<IngestPanel onLoaded={onLoaded} />);
78	
79	    pickFile(videoFile(0.3 * MB));
80	    expect(screen.getByTestId("ingest-source").textContent).toContain("match.mp4");
81	
82	    // Flush the upload chain (init → part → complete) + the process submit → schedules the poll.
83	    await flush();
84	    // The uploaded server PATH (not the URI) is what gets processed.
85	    expect(processBody?.video_path).toBe("/srv/uploads/match.mp4");
86	    expect(screen.getByTestId("ingest-source").textContent).toContain("match.mp4");
87	    expect(screen.getByText(/Processing your video.*match\.mp4/i)).toBeTruthy();
88	
89	    // Poll tick → done → the job's canonical video_id is handed up.
90	    await act(async () => {
91	      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1000);
92	    });
93	    await flush();
94	    expect(onLoaded).toHaveBeenCalledWith("VIDFINAL", "match.mp4");
95	  });
96	});

1	// i18n scaffold (tracker B1) — the shared localization foundation for KhelSutra Studio.
2	//
3	// Per docs/archives/FRIENDLY_AND_MULTILINGUAL.md + the engine's I18N_PLAN.md:
4	//   - keys, never prose (t("app.title"), enforced later by a no-bare-strings lint, B2);
5	//   - ICU MessageFormat for interpolation/plurals (Kannada/Hindi plural rules live in the catalog,
6	//     not the code — replaces the SPA's hand-rolled `count > 1 ? "s" : ""`);
7	//   - one negotiation order shared with the marketing site (B3);
8	//   - always-fallback to `en` so a missing translation degrades to English, never a raw key.
9	import i18n from "i18next";
10	import { initReactI18next } from "react-i18next";
11	import LanguageDetector from "i18next-browser-languagedetector";
12	import ICU from "i18next-icu";
13	// Catalogs are HOISTED to repo-root /locales (D-CATALOG-HOME) — the single source of truth shared
14	// by this SPA and the marketing site's t() shim (tracker B3). Vite resolves the workspace root via
15	// the repo `.git` marker, so importing the parent-dir /locales works in dev, build, and vitest.
16	import enCommon from "../../../locales/en/common.json";
17	import knCommon from "../../../locales/kn/common.json";
18	import hiCommon from "../../../locales/hi/common.json";
19	import esCommon from "../../../locales/es/common.json";
20	import daCommon from "../../../locales/da/common.json";
21	import idCommon from "../../../locales/id/common.json";
22	
23	// Supported locales: en/kn/hi foundation (FRIENDLY D6) + MULTILINGUAL_EXPANSION Phase A (es/da/id,
24	// Latin, catalog-only). Anything else falls back to `en`.
25	export const SUPPORTED_LOCALES = ["en", "kn", "hi", "es", "da", "id"] as const;
26	export type Locale = (typeof SUPPORTED_LOCALES)[number];
27	export const DEFAULT_LOCALE: Locale = "en";
28	
29	// ONE persisted-preference key, shared with the marketing site's t() shim (D9 / tracker B3), so a
30	// visitor's language choice carries across both surfaces.
31	export const LANG_STORAGE_KEY = "khelsutra.lang";
32	
33	// Catalogs live in repo-root /locales (D-CATALOG-HOME), consumed by both surfaces. `kn` + `hi`
34	// shipped (B5/B6, AI-drafted → native review pending). A missing key in any locale still falls back
35	// to `en` — the by-design invariant.
36	const resources = {
37	  en: { common: enCommon },
38	  kn: { common: knCommon },
39	  hi: { common: hiCommon },
40	  es: { common: esCommon },
41	  da: { common: daCommon },
42	  id: { common: idCommon },
43	} as const;
44	
45	void i18n
46	  .use(ICU)
47	  .use(LanguageDetector)
48	  .use(initReactI18next)
49	  .init({
50	    resources,
51	    fallbackLng: DEFAULT_LOCALE,
52	    supportedLngs: [...SUPPORTED_LOCALES],
53	    nonExplicitSupportedLngs: true, // en-IN -> en
54	    defaultNS: "common",
55	    ns: ["common"],
56	    interpolation: { escapeValue: false }, // React already escapes
57	    returnNull: false,
58	    // Negotiation order (I18N_PLAN §2.3): persisted pref -> ?lang= -> browser -> en.
59	    detection: {
60	      order: ["localStorage", "querystring", "navigator"],
61	      lookupLocalStorage: LANG_STORAGE_KEY,
62	      lookupQuerystring: "lang",
63	      caches: ["localStorage"],
64	    },
65	    react: { useSuspense: false },
66	  });
67	
68	// Keep <html lang> in sync with the active locale (screen readers + SEO use it). Reflects the
69	// resolved content language, so an `en` fallback for an absent locale still reads as `en`.
70	function syncDocumentLang(lng: string) {
71	  if (typeof document !== "undefined") {
72	    document.documentElement.lang = (i18n.resolvedLanguage || lng || DEFAULT_LOCALE).split("-")[0];
73	  }
74	}
75	i18n.on("languageChanged", syncDocumentLang);
76	syncDocumentLang(i18n.language ?? DEFAULT_LOCALE);
77	
78	export default i18n;

234	        "body": "Even lighting, then record the <b>whole session</b> and upload it as-is — KhelSutra finds the rallies and does the cutting."
235	      },
236	      "checklistCta": "Full 1-page capture checklist →"
237	    },
238	    "capture": {
239	      "diagram": {
240	        "title": "Side view: where the camera goes",
241	        "camera": "Camera — behind the baseline, elevated",
242	        "angle": "Tilt ~30° down",
243	        "frame": "Whole court in frame — players never cropped",
244	        "net": "Net"
245	      },
246	      "title": "1-page capture checklist",
247	      "intro": "Great highlights start with a steady, full-court recording. Five minutes of setup makes a big difference to your reels.",
248	      "ruleLead": "The golden rule: one match, one court, in full frame — and don't move the camera.",
249	      "ruleRest": "Multi-court footage is the hard case we're still sharpening; a single court in frame gives the best results today.",
250	      "step1": {
251	        "title": "Place the camera",
252	        "b1": "Behind <b>one baseline</b>, elevated — a high tripod, a few metres up, or a balcony.",
253	        "b2": "Tilt down about <b>30°</b>; keep the <b>whole court</b> in frame with a little margin and net headroom.",
254	        "b3": "Players should never leave the frame."
255	      },
256	      "step2": {
257	        "title": "Settings",
258	        "b1": "<b>1080p or higher</b>, shot in <b>landscape</b>.",
259	        "b2": "<b>30 fps</b> for badminton/pickleball; <b>60 fps</b> for table tennis.",
260	        "b3": "<b>Audio ON</b> (don't mute) — keep the mic unobstructed, wind/noise reduction off."
261	      },
262	      "step3": {
263	        "title": "Keep it still",
264	        "b1": "Fixed tripod or mount.",
265	        "b2": "<b>No panning, no zooming</b> during play."
266	      },
267	      "step4": {
268	        "title": "Light & record",
269	        "b1": "Even indoor lighting; avoid backlight, glare and very dark halls.",
270	        "b2": "Record the <b>whole session</b> and upload it as-is — KhelSutra does the cutting."
271	      },
272	      "doTitle": "Do",
273	      "do1": "Fixed camera, full court, behind the baseline",
274	      "do2": "1080p+, landscape, audio on",
275	      "do3": "One match on one court",
276	      "dontTitle": "Avoid",
277	      "dont1": "Handheld or phone-in-hand",
278	      "dont2": "Broadcast-style pans & zooming",
279	      "dont3": "Several courts in one frame; very dark halls",
280	      "consent": "Please record adults with their okay. Footage involving minors is never used to improve the tool and is never published.",
281	      "questions": "Questions?",
282	      "printTip": "Tip: press Ctrl/Cmd + P to print or save as PDF."
283	    },
284	    "ownModel": {
285	      "requestAccess": "Request access →",
286	      "heroTitle": "Annotate your footage. <span class=\"hl\">Train a model that's yours.</span>",
287	      "heroLead": "You can't train Gemini or GPT on your court footage. KhelSutra's direction is the inverse — a small, <b style=\"color:#EAF3EE\">owned</b> rally model where the moat is your consented dataset, on a commercially-clean, inspectable stack you control.",
288	      "nav": {
289	        "home": "← KhelSutra home",
290	        "annotate": "Annotate",
291	        "train": "Train your own model",
292	        "setup": "Setup & use"
293	      },
294	      "sample": "▶ <b>Want to see what KhelSutra produces?</b> A real sample reel plays the moment you <a href=\"/#join\">request access</a>.",
295	      "annotate": {
296	        "kicker": "Step 1 · real and shipping today",
297	        "title": "Annotate your footage",
298	        "pill": "live today",
299	        "body": "<b>rally-annotator</b> is a free, open-source (MIT) <b>VLC plugin</b> that turns \"watch a match\" into golden labels. While you watch your own video in VLC, you mark each rally's start and end plus how the point ended; it writes one row per rally to a plain CSV next to your video. <b>Your footage never leaves your machine.</b> It covers five net-separated racquet sports (badminton, tennis, table tennis, pickleball, padel).",
300	        "cap1": "Enable it once: VLC → View → Rally Annotator.",
301	        "cap2": "Mark each rally's start/end + ending reason, then Save Rally.",
302	        "flowLead": "The flow, per rally:",
303	        "step1": "<b>Mark START</b> on the serve-contact frame (pause/scrub for accuracy).",
304	        "step2": "<b>Mark END</b> when the shuttle is dead.",
305	        "step3": "Pick an <b>ending reason</b>, optionally a shot count, then <b>Save Rally</b> — one CSV row.",
306	        "step4": "Fix anything with <b>Edit / Delete / Undo</b>; re-open the same video later to continue.",
307	        "note": "It's a click-driven dialog (no marking hotkeys yet), and targets <b>VLC 3.0.x</b>."
308	      },
309	      "train": {
310	        "kicker": "Step 2 · the moat",
311	        "title": "Train your own model",
312	        "pill": "open pipeline · in build",
313	        "reassureTitle": "You don't have to train anything to start.",
314	        "reassureBody": "KhelSutra runs on a ready-made default, so you get rally detection and reels <b>out of the box</b> — nothing to set up. Training on your own footage (below) is an <b>optional</b> power-up: a model tuned to your courts, your angles, your halls.",
315	        "intro": "For those who want it, your labels feed an <b>owned</b> rally model — trained on <b>your</b> courts, <b>your</b> angles, <b>your</b> busy multi-court halls. The pipeline is open and inspectable:",
316	        "moat": {
317	          "ownTitle": "You own it",
318	          "ownBody": "A model and dataset that are yours to keep and improve — not rented inference.",
319	          "cleanTitle": "Commercially clean",
320	          "cleanBody": "MIT/Apache/BSD-only stack; we never train on a paid LLM's output.",
321	          "privateTitle": "Private & consented",
322	          "privateBody": "Footage stays yours; training use is explicit opt-in, never automatic.",
323	          "minorsTitle": "Minors protected",
324	          "minorsBody": "Footage involving minors is never used for training and never published."
325	        },
326	        "honestTitle": "Read this honestly.",
327	        "honestBody": "The labeling tool is real and shipping. The training pipeline is <b>open and in active build</b> — today it's a developer CLI; the no-GPU path trains a pipeline-validation <b>mock</b>, and a real model needs a GPU. There is <b>no \"Train now\" button</b> yet, and we make <b>no accuracy claim vs a paid LLM</b> (there's no such benchmark). The promise here is <b>ownership, licensing, privacy and consent</b> — not \"more accurate than Gemini,\" and not \"one click to train.\""
328	      },
329	      "setup": {
330	        "kicker": "Step 3 · for power users",
331	        "title": "Setup & use",
332	        "install": "<b>Install the plugin (VLC 3.0.x).</b> Copy <code>rally_annotator.lua</code> into VLC's per-user Lua <code>extensions</code> folder (create it if missing), then VLC → Tools → Plugins and extensions → <i>Reload extensions</i>, and enable it from <i>View → Rally Annotator</i>:",
333	        "record": "<b>Record</b> per the <a href=\"/capture\">one-page capture checklist</a> (fixed tripod, full court, landscape, whole session) → <b>annotate</b> in VLC → you get <code>&lt;video&gt;.rallies.csv</code>:",
334	        "reasons": "<code>ending_reason</code> is one of: <code>unknown</code> · <code>winner</code> · <code>forced_error</code> · <code>unforced_error</code> · <code>service_fault</code> · <code>let</code> · <code>other</code>. Times are decimal seconds; downstream tools read by column name.",
335	        "train": "<b>Train</b> (developer CLI). A GPU-free dry run validates the whole pipeline on a CPU mock; a real model needs a GPU:",
336	        "joinRule": "One join rule: keep the video <b>byte-identical</b> to what you annotated (don't re-encode after labeling) — labels match videos by content hash."
337	      },
338	      "not": {
339	        "kicker": "So expectations stay honest",
340	        "title": "What this is not",
341	        "i1": "Not a one-click trainer — training is an offline CLI today.",
342	        "i2": "Not in-browser — labeling runs in VLC on your machine.",
343	        "i3": "Not an accuracy claim vs Gemini/GPT — no such benchmark.",
344	        "i4": "Shot-type, player and score detection are roadmap."
345	      },

355	    "how": {
356	      "kicker": "What it does today",
357	      "title": "A full match, turned into rallies you can search",
358	      "intro": "Upload a whole session or match. KhelSutra does the rest — no editing skill needed.",
359	      "indexed": {
360	        "title": "Every rally, indexed",
361	        "body": "It finds each rally — start, end and length — and turns the whole match into a searchable list. Long full-session videos are fine."
362	      },
363	      "deadtime": {
364	        "title": "Dead time removed",
365	        "body": "Knock-ups, walks, picking up the shuttle, the gaps between points — stripped out. What's left is only real play."
366	      },
367	      "reuse": {
368	        "title": "Process once, reuse",
369	        "body": "Come back later to watch or download your reel. Re-running a video replaces its earlier result rather than piling up copies."
370	      },
371	      "stitch": {
372	        "title": "Stitched reel + summary",
373	        "body": "Your selection becomes one MP4 to download and share — with a play-time summary and a small “Generated by Khelsutra.guru” mark."
374	      }
375	    },
376	    "diff": {
377	      "kicker": "The hard problem we crack",
378	      "title": "Most academy halls run several courts at once",
379	      "p1": "Academies already record a lot of video — and then almost never watch it, because cutting it into useful clips by hand is tedious. The good rallies stay buried.",
380	      "p2": "A single recording of a busy, multi-court hall is exactly where ordinary video tools choke. Pulling the <b style=\"color:var(--tx)\">real</b> rallies out of busy, multi-court footage is the hard problem KhelSutra is built for. It already works very well on clean, single-court recordings — and the multi-court case is what we're sharpening now.",
381	      "tipPill": "Capture tip",
382	      "tipTitle": "Best results: a steady, full-court shot",
383	      "tipBody": "Camera behind the court, slightly elevated, whole court in frame. Keep it still — a fixed tripod beats handheld every time. Shoot landscape, record the whole session, upload as-is. KhelSutra does the cutting."
384	    },
385	    "sharper": {
386	      "kicker": "What makes it different",
387	      "title": "It gets sharper from real academy footage",
388	      "intro": "The footage real academies contribute — especially the busy multi-court halls — is what makes KhelSutra better over time. Early users aren't just customers; they're co-builders.",
389	      "improve": {
390	        "title": "Improves with the library",
391	        "body": "When you choose to share footage and coaches confirm what it found, that growing labelled library is what sharpens future versions — rather than staying frozen."
392	      },
393	      "data": {
394	        "title": "Your data stays yours",
395	        "body": "We never use your video to improve the tool without your clear, explicit opt-in. We never publish your footage. You decide what, if anything, you share."
396	      },
397	      "minors": {
398	        "title": "Minors are protected",
399	        "body": "Footage involving minors is never used to improve the tool and is never published — full stop."
400	      },
401	      "honestLead": "Read this honestly:",
402	      "honestBody": "Today the tool gives you rally detection, reels, and summaries. It does not retrain itself as you use it, and there's no per-person tracking. \"Gets sharper over time\" is the model we're building toward, powered by contributed footage — not a switch that's already on."
403	    },
404	    "ask": {
405	      "kicker": "Coming next to the alpha",
406	      "title": "Soon: ask for the exact reel you want",
407	      "intro": "Your match is already a clean index of rallies — so the natural next step is to let you build any reel just by describing it, in plain English. This is rolling out to alpha users.",
408	      "q1": "“Every rally over 10 seconds”",
409	      "a1": "6 rallies · a 1:32 reel of the long, end-to-end battles",
410	      "q2": "“Just the third game, longest first”",
411	      "a2": "12 rallies, reordered by length — that game only",
412	      "q3": "“The 8 best rallies from today”",
413	      "a3": "an 8-clip highlight from your latest session",
414	      "note": "<b style=\"color:var(--tx)\">Rolling out now.</b> Targeted asks — by rally length, count, order, or which game — come first; shot-type and player queries follow as the deeper analysis arrives."
415	    },
416	    "roadmap": {
417	      "kicker": "Where it's going",
418	      "title": "Toward an AI assistant coach",
419	      "intro": "Fed by the growing video library, KhelSutra is heading from \"finds rallies\" to a genuine training companion. This is the direction — not today's buttons.",
420	      "shot": {
421	        "title": "Shot detection",
422	        "body": "Smashes, drops, net shots, serves and clears within each rally."
423	      },
424	      "deep": {
425	        "title": "Deeper analysis",
426	        "body": "Per-rally and per-match patterns — what's working and what isn't."
427	      },
428	      "history": {
429	        "title": "Per-match history",
430	        "body": "Track progress over weeks and months, not just one clip."
431	      },
432	      "coach": {
433	        "title": "Assistant coach",
434	        "body": "Surfaces what to work on next — supports the real coach, never replaces."
435	      },
436	      "more": {
437	        "title": "More sports & languages",
438	        "body": "Table tennis next; Kannada and Hindi alongside English."
439	      }
440	    },
441	    "not": {
442	      "kicker": "So expectations stay honest",
443	      "title": "What KhelSutra is not",
444	      "li1": "An early, invite-only trial — useful, but still improving. You'll hit rough edges.",
445	      "li2": "Not a line-calling, scoring, or umpiring system.",
446	      "li3": "Not player tracking or identification.",
447	      "li4": "Not a replacement for a coach — a tool that saves time and adds insight.",
448	      "li5": "Shot detection and deeper analytics are on the roadmap, not available today.",
449	      "li6": "Pricing isn't set — today's invited use is about getting it right."
450	    },
451	    "faq": {
452	      "kicker": "Quick answers",
453	      "title": "Common questions",
454	      "q1": {
455	        "q": "How accurate is it?",
456	        "a": "Very good on a clean, single-court recording. A busy multi-court hall is harder — and is exactly what we're improving. The best answer is to show you a sample reel of your own court rather than quote a number."
457	      },
458	      "q2": {
459	        "q": "Can I choose which rallies go in the reel?",
460	        "a": "Today your reel includes the rallies KhelSutra found, with the dead time removed. Choosing by length, count, order or which game — just by asking in plain English — is rolling out to alpha users next; shot-type and player queries follow as the deeper analysis arrives."
461	      },
462	      "q3": {
463	        "q": "Do I need a special camera or hardware?",
464	        "a": "No. A phone or GoPro on a tripod, behind the court, whole court in frame. We provide a one-page capture checklist at setup."
465	      },
466	      "q4": {
467	        "q": "Is my video private? What happens to it?",
468	        "a": "We treat your footage as private: we never publish it, and we never use it to improve the tool without your explicit opt-in. These are large videos that need real processing, so your video is handled securely on our infrastructure to make your reel. Footage involving minors is never used and never published."
469	      },
470	      "q5": {
471	        "q": "Does it work for doubles and tournaments?",
472	        "a": "It finds rallies in doubles too. Tournament footage varies — a clean, fixed, full-court recording works well; crowded multi-court tournament halls are the hard case we're specifically working on."
473	      },
474	      "q6": {
475	        "q": "Which sports?",
476	        "a": "Badminton today. Table tennis and other racket sports are on the roadmap."
477	      },
478	      "q7": {
479	        "q": "How do I join?",
480	        "a": "It's an invite-only private trial. Just <a href=\"#join\">request access</a> using the form — it takes a minute, you'll see a real sample reel right away, and we'll be in touch about recording your own court."
481	      }

492	      "back": "← Back to KhelSutra",
493	      "title": "Privacy Policy",
494	      "sub": "How KhelSutra handles your information and your match videos — and the choices you have.",
495	      "updated": "Last updated: 22 June 2026",
496	      "lead": "KhelSutra (\"we\", \"us\") turns a full badminton match into a clean highlight reel. We're an invite-only alpha run from Bengaluru, India. This page explains, in plain language, what information we collect, how we use it, who else touches it, and how you stay in control. Questions? Email <a href=\"mailto:hello@khelsutra.guru\">hello@khelsutra.guru</a>.",
497	      "tocHeading": "On this page",
498	      "toc": {
499	        "collect": "What we collect",
500	        "use": "How we use it",
501	        "video": "Your videos & how they're processed",
502	        "sharing": "Who else touches your data",
503	        "cookies": "Cookies & analytics",
504	        "retention": "How long we keep it",
505	        "rights": "Your choices & rights",
506	        "minors": "Minors",
507	        "security": "Security",
508	        "changes": "Changes & contact"
509	      },
510	      "collect": {
511	        "h": "1. What we collect",
512	        "intro": "We only collect what we need to run the alpha and produce your reels:",
513	        "li1": "<b>Request-access details</b> — when you fill in the access form: your name and email, and optionally your city, academy/club name, how many courts you record, whether you have a powerful (GPU) computer, where your videos live, and any message you send us.",
514	        "li2": "<b>Match videos you choose to give us</b> — the footage you upload or share so we can index the rallies and build your reel.",
515	        "li3": "<b>Basic technical data</b> — like your approximate country and browser type, handled by our hosting and edge-security provider (Cloudflare) to keep the site working and safe.",
516	        "note": "We do <b>not</b> ask for, or want, sensitive personal data, and we don't run intrusive cross-site advertising trackers."
517	      },
518	      "use": {
519	        "h": "2. How we use it",
520	        "li1": "To <b>contact you about the alpha</b> — but only if you ticked the consent box on the form.",
521	        "li2": "To <b>provide the service</b> — to index your match, strip the dead time, and generate the reel you download.",
522	        "li3": "To <b>understand demand and improve the product experience</b> in aggregate (e.g. how many academies vs. individuals are interested).",
523	        "note": "We do <b>not</b> sell, rent, or trade your personal data. We do <b>not</b> use your footage to train or improve our models <b>unless you give us clear, explicit, opt-in permission</b> — and even then, never footage involving minors."
524	      },
525	      "video": {
526	        "h": "3. Your videos & how they're processed",
527	        "intro": "Your match videos are large files that need real computing to analyse, so here's exactly what happens to them:",
528	        "li1": "We process your video on our own infrastructure to find the rallies and build your reel.",
529	        "li2": "As part of the analysis we may use <b>trusted third-party AI and cloud services</b> (for example, Google's Gemini API) to detect rallies. When we do, we send only the footage needed for that step.",
530	        "li3": "We <b>never publish</b> your footage, and we never make it public.",
531	        "li4": "We never use your footage to improve the tool without your explicit opt-in (see above).",
532	        "boxTitle": "If you run your own model:",
533	        "boxBody": "The \"own your model\" path lets advanced users process footage on their own hardware. In that case your video stays on your machine and is not sent to us or to third-party AI services."
534	      },
535	      "sharing": {
536	        "h": "4. Who else touches your data",
537	        "intro": "We keep the list of processors small and name them honestly:",
538	        "li1": "<b>Cloudflare</b> — hosts the website, secures it at the edge, and provides privacy-friendly analytics.",
539	        "li2": "<b>Google (Gemini API)</b> — used, when applicable, to analyse footage and detect rallies.",
540	        "li3": "<b>Email delivery</b> — to send the messages you asked us to send about the alpha.",
541	        "note": "These providers act on our behalf to deliver the service. We don't give anyone your data to use for their own marketing."
542	      },
543	      "cookies": {
544	        "h": "5. Cookies & analytics",
545	        "intro": "We keep this light:",
546	        "li1": "We store a single <b>language preference</b> in your browser so the site remembers the language you picked. That's a functional preference, not a tracking cookie.",
547	        "li2": "We use <b>privacy-first analytics</b> (Cloudflare Web Analytics) to count visits and see which pages are useful. It does not use tracking cookies and does not build a profile of you across other sites."
548	      },
549	      "retention": {
550	        "h": "6. How long we keep it",
551	        "body": "We keep your information and footage only as long as we need it to run the alpha and provide the service, and then delete or anonymise it. You can ask us to delete your uploaded videos and your account details at any time — see your rights below."
552	      },
553	      "rights": {
554	        "h": "7. Your choices & rights",
555	        "intro": "You're in control. At any time you can ask us to:",
556	        "li1": "See what personal data we hold about you;",
557	        "li2": "Correct anything that's wrong;",
558	        "li3": "Delete your data and uploaded footage;",
559	        "li4": "Withdraw your consent to be emailed (every email we send also has an easy opt-out).",
560	        "note": "To exercise any of these, just email <a href=\"mailto:hello@khelsutra.guru\">hello@khelsutra.guru</a> and we'll take care of it."
561	      },
562	      "minors": {
563	        "h": "8. Minors",
564	        "body": "We take footage involving minors seriously. Footage involving minors is <b>never</b> used to improve the tool and is <b>never</b> published — full stop. If you believe a minor's personal data has been shared with us inappropriately, contact us and we'll remove it."
565	      },
566	      "security": {
567	        "h": "9. Security",
568	        "body": "The site is served only over <b>HTTPS</b> with an auto-renewing TLS certificate, so your connection is encrypted. The alpha app itself is gated behind an authenticated, invite-only sign-in, and access to the processing system is restricted. No system is perfectly secure, but we take reasonable, layered measures to protect your data."
569	      },
570	      "changes": {
571	        "h": "10. Changes & contact",

573	      },

1	// Typed client for the engine FastAPI. Default = SAME-ORIGIN relative `/api/*` (the single-origin
2	// appliance reverse-proxies UI + engine behind one origin; in dev Vite proxies `/api` → :8000).
3	// Every call carries a correlation id (header + logs) so a journey is traceable end to end.
4	import { log, newCorrelationId } from "../lib/log";
5	import type {
6	  RallySegment,
7	  QueryResponse,
8	  CompileResult,
9	  EngineConfig,
10	  EngineCapabilities,
11	  ProcessResponse,
12	  JobStatus,
13	  UploadInitResponse,
14	  UploadPartResponse,
15	  UploadCompleteResponse,
16	  VideoSummary,
17	  VideosResponse,
18	  AnnotationsResponse,
19	  AssetResponse,

21	
22	/** Resolve the engine API base. Default same-origin `"/api"`. For a SPLIT deploy — SPA on
23	 *  Cloudflare Pages + the engine on a separate `api.*` cloudflared tunnel (HOSTING_PLAN.md §2) —
24	 *  set `VITE_API_BASE` at build time to the engine's API base **including the `/api` path**
25	 *  (e.g. `https://api.khelsutra.guru/api`), and lock the engine's `RALLY_CORS_ALLOW_ORIGINS` to the
26	 *  Pages origin + put CF Access in front of both hostnames. A trailing slash is trimmed so
27	 *  `` `${base}/process` `` never double-slashes. */
28	export function apiBase(): string {
29	  return (import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE ?? "/api").replace(/\/+$/, "");
30	}
31	
32	const BASE = apiBase();
33	
34	export class ApiError extends Error {
35	  readonly cid?: string;
36	  readonly status?: number;
37	  readonly body?: string;
38	  constructor(message: string, opts: { cid?: string; status?: number; body?: string; cause?: unknown } = {}) {
39	    super(message);
40	    this.name = "ApiError";
41	    this.cid = opts.cid;
42	    this.status = opts.status;
43	    this.body = opts.body;
44	    if (opts.cause !== undefined) (this as { cause?: unknown }).cause = opts.cause;
45	  }
46	}
47	
48	interface RequestOpts {
49	  method?: string;
50	  body?: string;
51	  headers?: Record<string, string>;
52	  /** Correlation id to thread through this call; auto-generated if absent. */
53	  cid?: string;
54	}
55	
56	async function request<T>(path: string, opts: RequestOpts = {}): Promise<T> {
57	  const cid = opts.cid ?? newCorrelationId();
58	  const url = `${BASE}${path}`;
59	  const method = opts.method ?? "GET";
60	  log("info", "api.request", { cid, method, url });
61	
62	  let res: Response;
63	  try {
64	    res = await fetch(url, {
65	      method,
66	      body: opts.body,
67	      headers: { "content-type": "application/json", "x-correlation-id": cid, ...(opts.headers ?? {}) },
68	    });
69	  } catch (err) {
70	    // Loud failure, never silent (LOCAL_APPLIANCE_ARCHITECTURE.md §5).
71	    log("error", "api.network_error", { cid, method, url, error: String(err) });
72	    throw new ApiError(`Network error calling ${method} ${url}`, { cid, cause: err });
73	  }
74	
75	  if (!res.ok) {
76	    const body = await res.text().catch(() => "");
77	    log("error", "api.http_error", { cid, method, url, status: res.status, body: body.slice(0, 500) });
78	    throw new ApiError(`${method} ${url} failed: ${res.status}`, { cid, status: res.status, body });
79	  }
80	
81	  const data = (await res.json()) as T;
82	  log("info", "api.response", { cid, method, url, status: res.status });
83	  return data;
84	}
85	
86	/** Submit a video (a `gs://`/`gcs://`/`s3://` bucket URI, or a path on the engine host) for

88	 *  (unsupported scheme / out-of-allowed-root) or 404 (missing LOCAL file) — surfaced as ApiError. */
89	export async function processVideo(

98	  return request<ProcessResponse>("/process", {
99	    method: "POST",

112	export async function listVideos(cid?: string): Promise<VideoSummary[]> {

135	 *  Exactly ONE source: an `uploadedPath` (a just-uploaded local file — a durable MD5-keyed copy is

137	 *  at rest, registered as-is). Feed `stored_uri` into processVideo(). Fast-fails 400 (bad medium /
138	 *  both-or-neither source), 403 (hosted posture), 404 (missing file), 507 (no space), 502 (put) —
139	 *  surfaced as ApiError. */
